# Names applied from the ff9-ds port's evidence (branch worktree-agent-a52060f77a83c91a1), for re-applying
# after merges. Each "## bin" section is a `python3 tools/rename.py --bin BIN --from FILE` list ("OLD NEW  # why").
#
# Before the main section, the BIOS stub chunks were split by adding type:func placeholders to
# config/symbols.us.txt (func_80063118, func_80065218/28/38, func_80065E70/80/90/A0/B0), INCLUDE_ASM lines for them
# in src/main/main.c, matching lines in config/library_funcs.us.txt, then progress.py --write-index.
# func_80065238 -> strcpy needs --force (the builtin name is already used in ovl_39), the second strcpy prototype in
# ovl_39.h was dropped and ovl_64.h's became `char *strcpy(char *, const char *)` (cast at its one call).
# func_80016BA0 -> _bu_init is not in the function index: symbol line added and the C use renamed by hand.
# The ovl_0a/14/15 sections name references to ovl_64 functions inside those overlays (symbol lines added to their
# own symbols files, src and header renamed), since rename.py only renames inside ovl_64.
#
# Struct fields (FIELDS at the end): rename members in the header, then tools/rename_fields.py, one struct per run.
# Types renamed afterwards: Unk800761D0 -> CdLoadState, Unk8C8 -> PreloadQueue, Unk80021938 -> PreloadEntry,
# Unk800577EC -> AkaoVoiceRegs.

## main: main (BIOS stubs and library wrappers)

func_80021320 CdMode  # [code] returns the byte CD_cw stores for command 0x0E (Setmode); ff9-ds port/psx/cd.c

func_800645C4 ReadGeomScreen  # [code] cfc2 GTE H ($26); ff9-ds port/psx/libgte.c
func_80018750 SpuGetReverbModeType  # [code] stores 0x8006798C, the mode SpuSetReverbModeType saves, through its argument

## main: main (AKAO sound driver)

func_80057640 akao_setVoiceVolume  # [code] voice regs +0/+2 (volume L/R), scaled by vol/128 when vol != 0; ff9-ds port/game/spu_regs.c
func_80057688 akao_setVoicePitch  # [code] voice reg +4 (sample rate); ff9-ds port/game/spu_regs.c
func_800576A0 akao_setVoiceStartAddr  # [code] voice reg +6 = SPU RAM byte address >> 3; ff9-ds port/game/spu_regs.c
func_800576BC akao_setVoiceLoopAddr  # [code] voice reg +0xE (repeat address) = byte address >> 3; ff9-ds docs/decomp-requests.md R11

func_800576F0 akao_setVoiceAdsr2  # [code] voice reg +0xA; ff9-ds port/game/spu_regs.c
func_80057708 akao_setVoiceAttack  # [code] ADSR1 bits 8-15 (attack rate, mode >> 2 as the exponential bit), keeps the low byte
func_80057738 akao_setVoiceDecay  # [code] ADSR1 bits 4-7 (decay shift)
func_80057760 akao_setVoiceSustainLevel  # [code] ADSR1 bits 0-3 (sustain level)
func_80057788 akao_setVoiceSustain  # [code] ADSR2 bits 6-15 (sustain rate, mode >> 1 as direction/exponential), keeps the release bits
func_800577BC akao_setVoiceRelease  # [code] ADSR2 bits 0-5 (release rate, mode >> 2 as the exponential bit)
func_800577EC akao_writeVoice  # [code] writes volume, pitch, start, ADSR and loop address from the channel's voice block and clears its dirty flags; ff9-ds docs/decomp-requests.md R11 (bulk voice setup)
func_80057898 akao_flushVoice  # [code] writes only the registers whose dirty bits are set (0x10 pitch, 3 volume, 0x80 start, 0x10000 loop, ADSR) through the akao_setVoice* writers
func_8005CDE4 akao_applyCdVolume  # [code] writes the high half of D_80080AC0 to SPU CD volume L/R 0x1F801DB0; ff9-ds port/game/spu_regs.c
func_8005B2D8 akao_cmdSetCdVolume  # [code] sound command 0x70 (g_akaoCommandHandlers) - sets the CD volume and cancels a fade
func_8005B30C akao_cmdFadeCdVolume  # [code] sound command 0x71 - CD volume step toward the target over n ticks, applied by func_8005CF04
func_8005B374 akao_cmdFadeCdVolumeFrom  # [code] sound command 0x72 - as 0x71 but from an explicit start volume
func_8005C864 akao_cmdNop  # [code] empty handler, fills the unused slots of g_akaoCommandHandlers
func_8005C86C akao_setReverbMode  # [code] SpuSetReverbModeType(mode | 0x100) between SpuSetReverb(0/1) unless the mode is already set
func_8005681C akao_initState  # [code] called once by akao_init - resets sequencer/channel state, main volume 0x3FFF, CD volume 0x7FFF, reverb mode 4, every voice's loop address; ff9-ds port/game/spu_regs.c
func_8005DFE0 akao_calcPitch  # [code] SPU pitch for a note from the instrument's base note and tuning, fine-tune result through out; ff9-ds docs/decomp-requests.md F25 (fine pitch argument)
D_8006F410 g_akaoSemitonePitch  # [code] 4096 * 2^(n/12) for the 12 semitones (u32), read by akao_calcPitch
D_80080AC0 g_akaoCdVolume  # [code] CD volume, 16.16 - the high half (D_80080AC2) goes to the SPU in akao_applyCdVolume
D_80080AC2 g_akaoCdVolumeHi  # [code] high half of g_akaoCdVolume, written to SPU CD volume L/R
D_80080A28 g_akaoCdVolumeStep  # [code] per-tick CD volume change set by akao_cmdFadeCdVolume*
D_80080A2C g_akaoCdVolumeFadeTicks  # [code] ticks left of the CD volume fade, counted down by func_8005CF04

## main: main (MBG player state used by several binaries)
D_80071E32 g_mbgState  # [code] 0 off, 1 after MBG_init, >=2 playing (mbg_play ++, mbg_stop --, mbg_update needs >=2); ff9-ds port/game/movie.c
D_80071E34 g_mbgFrame  # [code] frames decoded, ++ in mbg_update, compared with mbg_getLastFrame by ovl_0a/ovl_14; ff9-ds port/game/movie.c
D_80071E31 g_mbgRgb24  # [code] -1 undecided (movie entry decides), 0 16-bit, 1 24-bit display (mbg_setRgb24, mbg_mdecOutCallback)
D_80071E38 g_mbgPaused  # [code] set at frame end on a pause request, frames then re-delivered by mbg_streamUngetFrame; cleared by mbg_setPause(0)

## main: main (game code)
func_800127A0 game_main  # [code] C main after __main - boot init, then per-session init and the g_gameState->mode dispatch loop until GameState.unk0 bit 0 (src/main/main.c; ff9-ds port/psx/mem.c calls it as the game's main loop, decomp-requests R3)
func_80012900 game_loadModeOverlay  # [code] mode -> file id table {20,10,11,12,21,-,-,101,101,13}, restores it from the preload cache (RAM copy or StoreImage from VRAM) else cd_readSync from FF9.IMG, then FlushCache (game_main; ff9-ds decomp-requests F3)
func_800169B8 game_initLibraries  # [code] once before the loop - ResetCallback, ResetGraph, InitGeom, SpuInit, InitCARD/StartCARD, cd_init (game_main)
func_80022B18 cd_getState  # [code] returns g_cdLoad.unk0, nonzero while a request runs; every caller spins on it (func_80012900, func_80022D84, func_80022DF0; ff9-ds scenario.c uses it as busy)
func_80022C60 cd_readAsync  # [code] (sector, size, dest, callback) fills the static g_cdReadRequest (flags 3 = seek+read, inline chunk) and submits it (func_80021ED4; ff9-ds scenario.c, decomp-requests R10)
func_80022DF0 cd_readSync  # [code] same request as cd_readAsync without callback, then spins on cd_getState (game_loadModeOverlay)
func_80022CC4 cd_readChunks  # [code] read into a list of n {size, dest} chunks, total size summed (CdChunk, cd_onSectorReady)
func_80022C10 cd_seekAsync  # [code] request flags 1 only (CdlSetloc, then cd_startRequest's seek with no read), with a done callback
func_80022D84 cd_seekSync  # [code] same seek-only request, then spins on cd_getState
func_800230B8 cd_submit  # [code] starts the request with cd_startRequest when idle, else queues one in g_cdLoad.unk968 (unk2 = queued) and returns 0 if already queued (all cd_read*/cd_seek* wrappers)
func_80022AC4 cd_findFile  # [string] [code] CdSearchFile + CdPosToInt, prints "Cannot find file. (%s)" and returns -1 on failure
func_80022A28 cd_init  # [code] loops CdInit until it succeeds, then CdControlB(CdlSetmode, CdlModeSpeed) (game_initLibraries)
func_80022A80 cd_resetState  # [code] CdReset(1), clears both libcd callbacks and memsets g_cdLoad (per-session init func_80016A28)
func_80022B28 cd_cancel  # [code] if busy, clears callbacks/flags, sets g_cdLoad idle and issues CdlPause (9) until accepted; returns the old state
func_80022BD0 cd_stopMotor  # [code] CdControlB(CdlSetmode, 0) then CdControlB(CdlStop)
func_800227C8 cd_onDataReady  # [code] ready callback installed by every read request - checks CdGetSector's header position is the expected next sector, calls cd_onSectorReady, pauses on the last sector or retries on error
func_800231A0 cd_onSync  # [code] sync callback installed by every request (unk10) - stores the result bytes in g_cdResult, dispatches on command 2/5 through D_80068668[state]
func_80023580 cd_controlF  # [code] plain CdControlF(com, param) wrapper used for every CD command in the module
func_8002306C cd_checkShellOpen  # [code] CdControlB(CdlGetstat) into g_cdResult, returns status bit 4 (CdlStatShellOpen)
D_80076148 g_cdReadRequest  # [code] static CdRequest filled by cd_readAsync and cd_readSync
D_80076B0C g_cdResult  # [code] last CD result bytes, written by cd_onSync/cd_onDataReady/cd_checkShellOpen
func_8001E218 file_find  # [code] (dir, id) - scans directory dir of g_gameState->unk14 backwards for the id, direct index for type-3 dirs, NULL if absent (game_loadModeOverlay; ff9-ds decomp-requests R10)
func_80021E5C preload_find  # [code] finds the preload queue entry (GameData.unk8C8, 7 entries) whose id matches the low 24 bits (game_loadModeOverlay, func_800220E8; ff9-ds decomp-requests F3 cached RAM/VRAM restore)
func_80021C60 preload_init  # [code] resets the preload queue (state 4, count 0, cursor -1) and clears all 7 entries (func_8001D268)
func_80021CFC preload_add  # [code] appends a cleared entry while count < 6 and returns it (ovl_0a/ovl_0b callers fill it)
func_8002246C preload_update  # [code] per-frame state machine - next entry, wait cd_getState, cd_readAsync/cd_seekAsync with preload_onReadDone, wait completion
func_800225BC preload_onReadDone  # [code] CD done callback of preload_update - marks the current entry done (|2) or failed (|0x80)
func_80019194 pad_update  # [code] per port - PadGetState, actuator setup, maps buttons into g_padPortHeld, then g_padPortPressed/g_padPortReleased from the previous frame (ff9-ds port/psx/pad.c)
D_80073930 g_padPortPrevHeld  # [code] g_padPortHeld from the previous pad_update, used for the press/release edges
D_80073988 g_padPortReleased  # [code] (held ^ prev) & prev per port in pad_update
D_800739A8 g_padPortState  # [code] PadGetState result per port in pad_update (2 = digital found, 6 = stable)
func_80027EDC item_findSlot  # [code] first inventory slot with count != 0 and this id, else NULL (item_getCount inlines it; ff9-ds scenario.c)
func_80033AF4 party_setMember  # [code] party[slot] = &characters[ch] or NULL for ch < 0 / >= 9, marks ch via func_80033BD0, flags a party change (func_80033690; ff9-ds scenario.c)

func_8003378C party_getFirstMember  # [code] index of the first non-NULL party slot
func_80032B08 chr_init  # [code] zeroes the record, initial CharBits from D_8006CA78[ch], default flags/name/equipment, model, level 1, full hp/mp/stones (func_80033690)

func_80033C48 chr_resetToLevel  # [code] level = 0 then chr_setLevel(ch, lv) so exp is reset, optional full heal, returns the old level (ff9-ds scenario.c)
func_80034A64 chr_calcBaseSpeed  # [code] result stored in Character.baseSpeed by chr_setLevel, capped 50

func_80034FD4 chr_calcMaxMp  # [code] (level, magic) table * mag / 100, capped 999, stored in maxMp by chr_setLevel
func_80033D38 chr_setDefaultName  # [code] fills the 10-byte name with 0xFF, copies default name id from the D_80010DC4 bank (chr_init; ff9-ds scenario.c, decomp-requests F22)
func_80033D8C chr_setDefaultEquip  # [code] copies the 5 equipment ids of set b from D_8006CAD8 (chr_init, func_800333B0; ff9-ds scenario.c)
func_80033A1C chr_getDefaultSet  # [code] D_8006CAB8[id][guest flag unkA&0x10] - the index chr_init passes to both chr_setDefaultName and chr_setDefaultEquip (ff9-ds scenario.c)
func_8004AEDC evt_getArg8  # [code] next instruction argument - evaluated expression if the g_evtArgFlags bit is set, else one literal byte; shifts the flags (evt_execInstr)
func_8004AF58 evt_getArg16  # [code] same with a 2-byte little-endian s16 literal
func_80052DA0 evt_evalExpr  # [code] runs the expression evaluator func_80051F54 on g_evtCurObj's ip and stores the advanced ip back (evt_getArg8)
func_80052D40 evt_popResult  # [code] pops the evaluator's result from the stack at g_evtExprSp via func_80051D4C (evt_getArg8)
func_80051F54 evt_runExpr  # [code] hand-written postfix expression evaluator - opcode byte at $s6 dispatched through 0x8006E718, pushes tagged words onto g_evtExprStack (src_equiv/main/script_text.c)
func_80051D4C evt_popValue  # [code] pops a tagged stack word and resolves it - immediate, object property, system var, or variable in g_evtGlobalVars / g_evtState / g_evtObjVars (src_equiv/main/script_text.c)
func_80051E8C evt_popAssign  # [code] pops a variable reference and stores a value into the same spaces via func_80051DF4 (src_equiv/main/script_text.c)
func_80051CA8 evt_readVar  # [code] reads base+off as bit, s24, s8/u8 or s16/u16 by the tag width bits
func_80051DF4 evt_writeVar  # [code] writes base+off with the same width encoding as evt_readVar
func_80052CD0 evt_getVarBank  # [code] bank 0 -> g_evtGlobalVars, 1 -> *g_evtState, 2 -> g_evtObjVars
D_8007AEF0 g_evtGlobalVars  # [code] variable space 0 of the script evaluator (evt_popValue, evt_getVarBank), 0x800 bytes cleared by script opcode 0xD1 (func_80050A84); h[0] is the story counter tested by chr_selectModel (ff9-ds scenario.c)
D_8007AECC g_evtState  # [code] pointer to the script system state (D_80077E60) - variable space 1, object lists, playerUid, party uids at unk16E4 (evt_execInstr, evt_popValue)
D_8007BCF8 g_evtObjVars  # [code] current object's local variable space, set to obj + obj->unk8*4 by func_80052870 (evt_popValue)
D_8007BCB8 g_evtExprStack  # [code] expression stack base loaded into $s7 by evt_runExpr
D_8007B6F0 g_evtExprSp  # [code] published copy of the evaluator's $s7 stack pointer (evt_popResult, evt_runExpr)
D_8007B6F4 g_evtArgFlags  # [code] the byte after the opcode - one bit per argument, set = expression (evt_execInstr, evt_getArg8)
D_8007B70C g_evtCurObj  # [code] object whose script is executing - its unk0 is the instruction pointer read by evt_execInstr, set by the scheduler func_80052870
D_8007AEA0 g_evtInstrStart  # [code] ip at the start of the current instruction, restored by evt_execInstr when an instruction must retry (case 0x12 returns 1)
D_8007B708 g_evtMode  # [code] evt_init copies g_gameState->mode 1/2/3 into it and 8 -> 4; selects message-table layout and field-only behaviour in evt_execInstr

## ovl_64: ovl_64 (MBG movie player)
func_801999B4 MBG_init  # [string] "Warning! MBG_init() called when already initialized"; carves MDEC/ring/audio buffers out of caller memory
func_80199B28 mbg_play  # [code] picks entry idx of movie list from D_8019DC5C, sets up MDEC buffers, ring, CD stream and decodes the first frame; ff9-ds port/game/movie.c
func_80199F30 mbg_update  # [code] per-frame: starts MDEC on the decoded frame, fetches/VLC-expands the next, returns 0 once stopped (called each frame by ovl_0a/14/15); ff9-ds port/game/movie.c
func_8019AAF8 mbg_stop  # [code] stops sound and CD stream, decrements the player state, calls the end callback, resets display; ff9-ds port/game/movie.c
func_8019A9D8 mbg_shutdown  # [code] inverse of MBG_init: clears all buffer pointers and the player state (callers end-callbacks in ovl_0a/14/15)
func_8019AA5C mbg_playToEnd  # [code] blocking loop calling mbg_update(1) until it returns 0
func_8019AAB4 mbg_setPause  # [code] nonzero requests a pause at the next frame end (mbg_mdecOutCallback), zero clears g_mbgPaused and restarts CD reading
func_8019AB70 mbg_getFrame  # [code] returns D_80071E34 (frames decoded) or -1 when not initialized; used by ovl_0a script query
func_8019AD0C mbg_getLastFrame  # [code] returns the stream's end frame (D_8019DE10.unk3C, from audio sector +0x60) or -1; callers compare D_80071E34 against it; ff9-ds port/game/movie.c
func_8019AC3C mbg_setEndCallback  # [code] stores D_8019DC94.unk60, called by mbg_stop
func_8019AC48 mbg_setScratchVlcTable  # [code] nonzero sets the VLC table to NULL = scratchpad (rodata "Using scratchpad"), else D_8019F288
func_8019AC6C mbg_getEntryCount  # [code] *out = D_8019DC5C[list].unk0 (entries in a movie list)
func_8019AD4C mbg_getStartCamera  # [code] copies the first frame's camera (D_8019F258/D_8019F278) to the caller; ovl_0a passes GameData.unk18 MATRIX
func_8019AE0C mbg_getGeomOffset  # [code] returns D_8019DC88, the GTE offset set from the frame camera record in func_801990A0
func_8019ABF0 mbg_getCameraSelect  # [code] wraps func_8019C99C (D_8019DE10.unk10)
func_8019AC14 mbg_setCameraSelect  # [code] wraps func_8019C9B4 (0/1 picks which of the two camera records func_8019BCE8 copies); ff9-ds docs/decomp-requests.md F28
func_8019A5B0 mbg_setRgb24  # [code] switches D_80071E31 / DecDCTin mode between 24-bit (0x1E0 halfword columns) and 16-bit (0x140); movie entry unk4 or ovl_0a/14 callers
func_8019A688 mbg_setDispEnv  # [code] SetDefDispEnv for one buffer, isrgb24 and 2/3 width in 24-bit mode, vertically centered height h
func_8019A7A8 mbg_setGraphEnv  # [code] sets both DISPENVs and DRAWENVs for 16/24-bit movie display (called with 0xE0 on mode change and stop)
func_8019AA20 mbg_putDispEnv  # [code] VSync(0), PutDispEnv(env), SetDispMask(1)
func_801990A0 mbg_readFrame  # [code] takes the next ready ring frame (func_8019C2F4), applies its camera record to GTE/GameData, copies mask data, flags end, updates frame size
func_80199554 mbg_mdecOutCallback  # [code] registered via DecDCToutCallback in mbg_play: applies mask, LoadImage of the decoded slice, starts the next DecDCTout
func_8019AE44 mbg_vlcBuildTable  # [runtime] ff9-ds docs/movie-validation.md original VLC setup run in PCSX-Redux (tools/test_movie_vlc_psx.py); [code] expands the 108 run/level pairs at D_8019DCFC into +/- MDEC halfwords, NULL = scratchpad
func_8019AE98 mbg_vlcSetSize  # [code] sets D_8019DDD4 output limit (n*2, <=0 means 0xFFFFFF) and returns the old one; func_8019AEC0 suspends at it; ff9-ds docs/decomp-requests.md F29
func_8019AEC0 mbg_vlcDecode  # [runtime] ff9-ds docs/movie-validation.md original routine compared byte-for-byte on 171 frames in PCSX-Redux; [code] BS v2/v3 to MDEC run-level, NULL input resumes from D_8019DDD8
func_8019B00C mbg_vlcDecodeBlock  # [code] register-ABI entry inside mbg_vlcDecode: stores the given coefficient, then decodes AC codes until EOB (FE00)
func_8019B188 mbg_vlcDecodeLumaBlockV3  # [code] register-ABI entry inside mbg_vlcDecode: v3 luma DC predictor (t9), jal'd 4x per macroblock, then mbg_vlcDecodeBlock
func_8019B3D4 mbg_applyMask  # [code] ORs D_8019DC84 (0x8000 STP) into the decoded 16x224 slice from RLE mask data (D_8019F4C8 cursor); E6000002 mask-check set in mbg_update
func_8019B7C4 mbg_drawStreamDebug  # [code] debug bar of the CD ring: one POLY_F4 per sector, colour per slot, brighter when ready (0x8000)
func_8019BA70 mbg_streamOnVideoSector  # [code] after CdGetSector of a 0x914-byte video sector into the ring (func_8019C434); checks frame number, marks slot ready; ff9-ds docs/decomp-requests.md F28
func_8019BCE8 mbg_streamOnAudioSector  # [code] after CdGetSector of a 2048-byte sector into the audio buffer; copies camera record/flags, end frame from +0x60; ff9-ds docs/decomp-requests.md F28
func_8019C1B0 mbg_streamStart  # [code] computes the start sector from base sector + (startFrame-1)*sectors per frame, resets counters, starts CD reading with func_8019C434
func_8019C2F4 mbg_streamGetFrame  # [code] returns the next ready ring slot's data/header/camera/mask; 3 not ready, 5 stream ended or failed
func_8019C434 mbg_streamReadyCallback  # [code] CD ready callback passed to func_80022F1C: reads the sector header, routes to audio or video sector handler, retries on timeout
func_8019C7E4 mbg_streamReset  # [code] returns 4 unless ring and audio buffers are set, else clears the stream status bytes and channel
func_8019C864 mbg_streamStop  # [code] clears the main-exe callback at D_80068254 via func_800213A0(0); only called by mbg_stop
func_8019C884 mbg_streamSetRing  # [code] ring of size/per slots (max 16): header array then 0x8F4-byte sector data, all slots marked free (0x1000)
func_8019C954 mbg_streamSetAudioBuffer  # [code] sets the 2048-byte-sector buffer (D_8019DE10.unk90) and its sector count
func_8019C98C mbg_streamSetBaseSector  # [code] sets D_8019DE10.unk1C, the movie's first sector used by mbg_streamStart
func_8019C99C mbg_streamGetCameraSelect  # [code] *out = D_8019DE10.unk10
func_8019C9B4 mbg_streamSetCameraSelect  # [code] D_8019DE10.unk10 = 0/1, selects s->unk14[0/1] in mbg_streamOnAudioSector
func_8019C9D4 mbg_streamPushAudio  # [code] when >=2 audio sectors are queued, consumes them and passes the stream to func_80056488
func_8019CA1C mbg_streamSeek  # [code] frees all slots, waits for CD idle, restarts reading at loc with mbg_streamReadyCallback
func_8019CA80 mbg_streamGetStartPos  # [code] copies the start CdlLOC D_8019DE10.unk20
func_8019CAC0 mbg_streamGetPos  # [code] CdIntToPos of the next sector D_8019DE10.unk44
func_8019CAEC mbg_streamPeekFrame  # [code] returns the read slot's data/header and its state (free/ready/0x2000/0x4000) without consuming
func_8019CBA0 mbg_streamCountFree  # [code] adds the number of free (0x1000) ring slots to *count
func_8019CBF4 mbg_streamGetLastFrame  # [code] *out = D_8019DE10.unk3C (end frame, 99999 until read from the stream)
func_8019CC0C mbg_streamUngetFrame  # [code] steps the read slot back so the same frame is returned again (used while g_mbgPaused)
func_8019CC3C mbg_streamSetPaused  # [code] nonzero pauses CD reading (func_80023018), zero restarts it at the current sector
func_8019CC7C mbg_streamClearRing  # [code] marks every ring slot free (0x1000)
func_8019CCBC mbg_streamFreeFrame  # [code] marks the slot holding data free and restarts CD reading if it had stalled on a full ring
D_8019DC94 g_mbgPlayer  # [code] player state: buffers (MBG_init), end callback, frame size, VLC table pointer

## R24 encounters
# Field/world-map encounter starts and battle backgrounds (docs/encounters.md, tools/encounter_catalog.py).

## main: main (R24 encounters: DB containers, dependency tables, random encounters, script battle opcodes)
func_8001C8B0 db_lookup  # [code] looks a key (kind << 16 | id) up in a 0xDB container - kind byte per block, u16 ids, u32 self-relative offsets; modes 0 block, 1 count, 2 address by index, 3 key by index, 4 address, 5 size (docs/encounters.md)
func_8001C7FC db_lookupRegistered  # [code] db_lookup over every container registered in g_dbList, newest first
func_8001C750 db_register  # [code] appends a container to g_dbList (fld_loadFile, btl_loadEncounterFile register the files they read)
func_8001C784 db_unregister  # [code] removes a container from g_dbList by swapping in the last one
D_80073C40 g_dbList  # [code] {u16, u16 count, containers[16]} searched by db_lookupRegistered (db_register, db_unregister)
func_80063088 dep_find  # [code] finds the entry with a 24-bit key in a dependency table {u16, u16 n, {key, sector, sectors, link}[n]} (GameData.unk7E4: battleEnd_init, btl_*)
func_80038D3C evt_rollEncounter  # [code] every 0x3C0 units walked adds the frequency to g_evtEncounterChance and starts a battle when a random byte is below chance >> 3 (field table g_evtEncounterTable, world ovl_0c 0x800BBBA8), evt_setNextBattle
D_8007AE40 g_evtEncounterTable  # [code] field random-encounter table {u16 battle[4], u8 pattern} written by script opcode 0x3C, read by evt_rollEncounter's pick
D_8007AE4C g_evtEncounterDist  # [code] distance walked since the last roll (func_80048F54 adds, evt_rollEncounter resets past 0x3C0, evt_init sets -0x5A0)
D_8007AE50 g_evtEncounterChance  # [code] accumulated encounter chance (evt_rollEncounter adds g_evtState->unk16EF per roll, compares chance >> 3; script opcode 0x57 with 0 clears it)
D_8007AE54 g_evtLastEncounter  # [code] previous random battle; evt_rollEncounter rerolls once when it picks the same
D_8007AE58 g_evtEncounterPending  # [code] set when evt_rollEncounter picked a battle, turned into event return value 3 (battle) by func_80048F54
D_8006E648 g_encounterSlotThresholds  # [code] per pattern (table byte & 3) three cumulative thresholds for one random byte choosing battle[0..3] (evt_rollEncounter's pick)
func_8001E2AC game_setNextScene  # [code] stores the destination id for the current mode - field scene unkA, battle unk11C0->unk4, world unk8->unk24 - read when the mode exits (fld_exit, btl_cleanup)
func_80021640 evt_setNextLocation  # [code] game_setNextScene for script opcodes 0x2B (field jump, returns 4) and 0xB6 (world map, returns 5)
func_80021660 evt_setNextBattle  # [code] game_setNextScene for script opcodes 0x2A/0x8C and evt_rollEncounter (event return value 3 = battle)

## ovl_0a: ovl_0a (R24 encounters: field exit and dependency lookups)
func_800A8E58 fld_exit  # [code] after the field loop: saves GameData.unk7FC/804 to unk7EC/7F4 and dispatches on the scene's exit reason (2 battle, 3 world map, 1 next field, 4/7/9 other modes)
func_800A9BDC fld_loadFile  # [code] reads the field file at GameData.unk7FC[1] (or its preload copy), sets GameData.unk7E4 to its dependency table (block 0x14) and registers the rest
func_800AA0F4 fld_depFindBattle  # [code] dependency-table entry 0x110000|battle -> encounter bundle sector/length, returns the key (fld_exit case 2)
func_800AA194 fld_depFindBackground  # [code] resolves an entry's link in the dependency table and returns the linked key with its sector/length - the battle background for fld_exit case 2
func_800AA290 fld_depFindField  # [code] dependency-table entry 0x50000|id -> field/world file sector/length (fld_exit cases 1 and 3)
func_800AA330 fld_depFindArea  # [code] resolves a field entry's link (its 0x06 area bundle) in the dependency table (fld_exit cases 1 and 3)

## ovl_0c: ovl_0c (R24 encounters: world-map dependency lookups and random-encounter tables)
func_800A9C1C wm_loadFile  # [code] reads the world file at GameData.unk7FC[1], sets GameData.unk7E4 to its dependency table (block 0x14)

func_800A9FC0 wm_depFindField  # [code] dependency-table entry 0x50000|id -> field file sector/length (world exit reasons 1 and 3)
func_800AA060 wm_depFindArea  # [code] resolves a field entry's link (0x06 area bundle) (world exit reasons 1 and 3)
func_800BBBA8 wm_getEncounterEntry  # [code] [string] "- Encount Information -" debug print; picks the random-encounter entry for the player's area -> zone, topography and fog (main evt_rollEncounter)
func_800BAD98 wm_applyEncounterEvents  # [code] for each set bit of event vars 0xC2 | 0xC6 << 8, copies battle[2] over battle[3] in the listed g_worldEncounterTable entries
D_800CF450 g_worldEncounterTable  # [code] 10-byte entries {u16 battle[4], u8 topography << 2 | pattern, u8 zone << 1 | fog}, world data pack entry 3 (func_800BA58C, wm_getEncounterEntry)
D_800D30D8 g_worldEncounterZoneStart  # [code] first g_worldEncounterTable entry per zone, 2 x running sum of g_worldZoneEncounterCount (func_800BA58C)
D_800CCFB4 g_worldAreaZone  # [code] [string] area (cell attribute bits 8-13) -> zone, printed as "Now Zone" by wm_getEncounterEntry
D_800CCFF4 g_worldZoneEncounterCount  # [code] half the number of g_worldEncounterTable entries per zone (func_800BA58C)
D_800CF444 g_worldDataSet  # [code] 1, or 4 when the story counter is >= 0x2B52 (func_800B69B4); selects world data 0x41B.. or 0x41E.. and the +0xFE encounter entries
D_800CCE4B g_worldFog  # [string] printed as "Now Fog" by wm_getEncounterEntry, matched against the encounter entries' fog bit
D_800D32E4 g_worldEncounterEventRows  # [code] world data pack entry 4: rows of 12 u16 entry numbers used by wm_applyEncounterEvents

### ovl_0a: bgi.c, tu_800C1E14.c (walkmesh)
func_800C1E14 bgi_segmentsCross  # [code] 2D cross-product test whether segment a0-a1 crosses segment a2-a3 on axes x/z; func_800C519C uses it to pick which of two crossed edges
func_800C1F98 bgi_getPassableNeighbor  # [code] returns triangle.neighbor[edge] if it has flag 1 and its mask byte doesn't block (0x80 blocks the player actor func_8004AE00, 0x40 everyone else), else NULL
func_800C20BC bgi_closestPointOnSegment  # [code] dot-product clamp of point a0 onto segment a1-a2, writes the point to a3, a4 = 1 unless clamped to an end, returns squared distance
func_800C2390 bgi_projectOnPlane  # [code] turns the move vector a1 into a vector in the plane of normal a0 (cross products with the horizontal perpendicular (-z,0,x)); used by bgi_slide per triangle
func_800C2524 bgi_pushOutOfEdges  # [code] for each triangle edge closer than the actor radius (unk20 = r^2) recurse into the passable neighbour or record a push vector of length r in D_800CA9D8; returns 1 when nothing hit
func_800C2920 bgi_findTriangleNear  # [code] point-in-triangle on the current triangle, then its passable neighbours, then recursively (depth < 9, flag 0x80 marks visited); updates *a1 and the actor's floor/triangle
func_800C2AF0 bgi_slide  # [code] moves an actor from a2 to a3 across the walkmesh, projecting the move onto each triangle's plane and following the neighbour across the crossed edge (bgi_findEdgeCrossing); recursive retries
func_800C31F8 bgi_applyPushes  # [code] adds the averaged distinct push vectors D_800CA9D8[D_800C9DDC] to the position and actor, clears the count, then re-runs bgi_slide from the old position
func_800C3608 bgi_moveActor  # [code] per-actor walkmesh step pos (unk40 -> pos), result bits 2 always, 4 not moved, 1 blocked (position reverted), 8 pushed, 0x10 corrected; sets the y from the triangle plane
func_800C3B80 bgi_init  # [code] fld_main.c passes the BGI file data +4 ("[+4 for file magic]" in bgi_dumpText); sets every D_800C9DF0 table pointer, resets the 24 actor entries, anims and floor moves
func_800C3D28 bgi_findPlayerActor  # [code] returns the D_800C9DF0.unk4 entry (and its index in *a0) whose actor is the player object (func_8004AE00 in mode 1, party leader in mode 5)
func_800C3E18 bgi_setActorActive  # [code] sets/clears actor-entry flag 1, clearing also resets floor/triangle (unk4..unkA) to -1; event opcode 0xA8, and opcodes 0x1D/0xA1 disable it around a teleport
func_800C3EC0 bgi_placeActor  # [code] scans every triangle containing (x,z), keeps the nearest height >= y, sets the actor's floor/triangle and position; called by bgi_updateActor when its triangle is -1
func_800C420C bgi_setActorTriangle  # [code] puts actor entry a0 on triangle a1 (floor from triangle.unk4) at the triangle centre (unk1E..22) + floor/mesh offset, pos and previous pos
func_800C4378 bgi_setActorFloorTriangle  # [code] as bgi_setActorTriangle but the triangle is entry a2 of floor a1's triangle index list (floor.unk1E)
func_800C44F4 bgi_updateActor  # [code] 0x8000 when no walkmesh, 0x4000 bad index, 0 inactive; active entries go to bgi_placeActor (triangle unknown) or bgi_moveActor; main func_8002170C returns this
func_800C45C0 bgi_findTriangleBelow  # [code] scans every triangle containing (x,z), returns 1 and the index/pointer of the one whose height is the smallest value >= y
func_800C47E4 bgi_setFloorActive  # [code] event opcode 0xCB (floor, on) - sets/clears flag 1 on the floor and on every triangle of its index list
func_800C48DC bgi_getHeight  # [code] height at (x,z) from the first triangle containing it (plane or first vertex); without a walkmesh the player actor's y, -0x7FFF when outside
func_800C4B30 bgi_pointInTriangle  # [code] crossing-number point-in-triangle test (edges count as inside); projects on x/y or y/z instead of x/z when the triangle's normal ny is small (steep)
func_800C4EC8 bgi_pointInTriangleXZ  # [code] the same test as bgi_pointInTriangle always on the x/z plane; used to find triangles under a point (bgi_placeActor, bgi_getHeight)
func_800C519C bgi_findEdgeCrossing  # [code] intersects the move segment a3-a4 with the triangle's edges (skipping edge a7), writes the hit point (with interpolated height) to a5 and the edge to a6
func_800C60F0 bgi_updateAnims  # [code] per-frame from fld_main - for running keyframe anims (flags 1 and 4/8) enables the current frame's triangles, swaps actors over (bgi_remapActors), advances "Current Frame" by "Frame Rate"
func_800C6408 bgi_showAnimFrame  # [code] event opcode 0xFF0A (anim, frame) - enables frame a1's triangles at once, disables the others, moves actors over and sets Current Frame
func_800C65CC bgi_transferActorPos  # [code] re-expresses the actor position on triangle n0 by barycentric weights and maps it onto triangle n1, height from n1's plane
func_800C6C04 bgi_remapActors  # [code] for every active actor on a triangle of the old frame, moves it to the triangle at the same index in the new frame (bgi_transferActorPos)
func_800C6DA4 bgi_linkFrameNeighbors  # [code] for the frame's triangles, writes their index into the neighbour's neighbour slot given by the edge's Edge Clone, and the clone back
func_800C6E94 bgi_resetFloorMoves  # [code] clears the 30 D_800CAE98 entries (flags 2, speed 0x100); called by bgi_init
func_800C6EEC bgi_stepFloorMove  # [code] advances one D_800CAE98 entry by its speed, linear or cosine curve (unk6), between min and max; at the end bounces (flag 0x10) or wraps and clears flag 4
func_800C7100 bgi_updateFloorMoves  # [code] per-frame from fld_main - sets each running mover's floor Current Position on its axis and moves the actors standing on that floor with it
func_800C72D8 bgi_setMesh  # [code] sets D_800C9DF0's table pointers from a BGI header like bgi_init, but keeps the actor entries; resets anims and floor moves; no callers
func_800C73AC bgi_updateActors  # [code] runs bgi_updateActor on every actor entry and returns how many returned > 0; no callers
func_800C7430 bgi_clear  # [code] fld_main.c, before bgi_init - clears the BGI header pointer, actor count/list and the loaded flag
func_800C7464 bgi_setMaskFilter  # [code] event opcode 0x27 - sets D_800C9DF0.unk1, which ANDs with the triangle mask byte (bgi_getPassableNeighbor, bgi_getTriangleFlags)
func_800C7474 bgi_getMaskFilter  # [code] reads D_800C9DF0.unk1 (bgi_setMaskFilter); no callers
func_800C748C bgi_getActor  # [code] &D_800C9DF0.unk4[i]; main event.c reads its unk8/unkA (last floor/triangle) and writes them on reload
func_800C74A8 bgi_bindActor  # [code] fld_movie.c - for slot < 9 clears entry a0 and points it at the object slot's actor
func_800C74F0 bgi_addActor  # [code] fld_movie.c - for slot >= 9 appends an entry (D_800C9DF0.unk2++) for the object slot's actor
func_800C7570 bgi_setActorRadius  # [code] event opcode 0x4B (value*4) - sets entry unk1C and unk20 = r*r, the radius bgi_pushOutOfEdges keeps from edges
func_800C75B4 bgi_getActorRadius  # [code] returns entry unk1C (bgi_setActorRadius); main event.c divides it by 4 back into the object
func_800C75E0 bgi_getActorRadiusSq  # [code] returns entry unk20 = radius squared; no callers
func_800C760C bgi_getActorNormal  # [code] returns the four words (nx, ny, nz, 1/ny) of the normal of the actor's triangle; no callers
func_800C768C bgi_getActorTilt  # [code] returns -Theta Z and Theta X of the actor's triangle; fld_movie.c uses them as the model's x/z rotation
func_800C76E8 bgi_getActorTriangle  # [code] returns the actor's triangle (unk6) and floor (unk4); main script variables 0x70/0x71 read them, tu_800B0124.c feeds the triangle to bgi_getTriangleFlags
func_800C7720 bgi_revertActor  # [code] restores pos from the previous position (unk40) and floor/triangle from unk8/unkA; main event.c calls it when bgi_updateActor reports bit 1 (blocked)
func_800C778C bgi_setTriangleActive  # [code] event opcode 0x9A (16-bit triangle, on) - sets/clears triangle flag 1
func_800C7800 bgi_getTriangleFlags  # [code] triangle flags word with the mask byte ANDed by D_800C9DF0.unk1; tu_800B0124.c takes the high byte
func_800C784C bgi_setTriangleMask  # [code] sets the triangle flags' high (mask) byte; no callers
func_800C7890 bgi_getTriangleMask  # [code] returns the triangle's high flag byte AND D_800C9DF0.unk1; no callers
func_800C78D8 bgi_getTriangleFloor  # [code] returns the triangle's Floor Index (unk4); no callers
func_800C7938 bgi_getEdgeFlags  # [code] returns the edge's Flags word (bgi_dumpText "Edge" record); no callers

func_80016A28 game_reset  # [code] start of every game_main loop pass: clears VRAM, resets CD, db, pad (pad_resetState), SPU reverb, then the subsystems' inits
func_80016B6C game_shutdown  # [code] end of a game_main loop pass (GameState.unk0 bit 0): stops sound and db, counts D_80067950
func_80018F50 pad_resetState  # [code] clears every per-port pad array and installs the default button mapping through pad_setMapping
func_80019708 pad_setMapping  # [code] D_80073730[port][bit index of b] = c: which raw controller buttons set game button b (pad_update ORs 1 << index)
func_80019150 pad_getFlags  # [code] returns D_80073720 (pad_update's per-port init bits, 0x8000 = analog stick acts as d-pad)
func_80019160 pad_setFlags  # [code] sets or clears bits a of D_80073720 (tu_8002BEEC toggles 0x8000)
func_80019750 pad_getAnalogThresholds  # [code] copies D_80068158, the four stick thresholds pad_update compares the analog bytes against for the d-pad bits
func_80019774 pad_setAnalogThresholds  # [code] sets D_80068158 (see pad_getAnalogThresholds)
D_80073720 g_padFlags  # [code] per port (4 bits each) init/actuator state in pad_update; 0x8000 maps the analog stick to the d-pad
D_80073730 g_padMapping  # [code] [port][game button bit] -> raw button mask, written by pad_setMapping, read by pad_update
D_80073830 g_padHoldFrames  # [code] [port][game button bit] frames held, counted in pad_update
D_80068158 g_padAnalogThresholds  # [code] stick thresholds {up, down, left, right} compared with the analog bytes in pad_update
D_80073938 g_padAnalog  # [code] per port the four analog stick bytes copied from the pad buffer in pad_update
D_80073940 g_padBuf  # [code] PadInitDirect receive buffers, one per port (pad_init)
D_80073998 g_padPortMask  # [code] per port game buttons masked out of g_padPortHeld in pad_update (battle sets 8, ovl_15 -1)
D_800739B0 g_padActuator  # [code] PadSetAct data per port in pad_update
D_8006815C g_padActAlign  # [code] PadSetActAlign table in pad_update
func_8001C6F8 db_init  # [code] game_reset: clears the registered-db list (db_clear)

func_8001C720 db_clear  # [code] zeroes g_dbList's counts and its 16 slots
func_8001CA70 db_getSize  # [code] checks the 0xDB magic byte, returns the end offset of the last entry's data (4 for an empty db)

func_8001CFB4 game_freezeScreen  # [code] before an overlay load (game_loadModeOverlay): shows the current buffer and fills the other with the fade colour while fading

func_80021698 evt_createModel  # [code] calls the mode's GameData.unk838 hook with db key obj.unk34 | 0x20000 (model); the result is the object's model slot unk3B (evt_execInstr, btl_init)
func_800216D8 evt_getAnimFrameCount  # [code] second u16 of the db type-3 (animation) entry; evt_execInstr stores it as the object's animation frame count

func_800219FC preload_isUsed  # [code] entry != NULL and fileId != -1
func_80021A1C preload_isBusy  # [code] entry flag 1: a CD request for it is running (set by preload_startRead)
func_80021A28 preload_isDone  # [code] kind 0: preload_isLoaded; kind 1 (seek): request finished without error
func_80021A8C preload_isLoaded  # [code] kind 0 entry fully read into RAM (flag 2 or 0x80, not busy, not in VRAM, has a destination)

func_80021B34 preload_makeChunk  # [code] builds the next read (queue slot 6) for an entry: continues at dest + bytes done, at most one chunk, whole sectors
func_80021C20 preload_wait  # [code] if the entry is busy, spins on func_80022D3C until the CD queue drains; returns 1 if it waited
func_80021CDC preload_setHook  # [code] sets PreloadQueue.unk11C, called first in preload_update (fld_main passes a field callback, others 0)
func_80021D7C preload_next  # [code] advances PreloadQueue.cur to the next used entry, marks it 0x40, returns it
func_80021E20 preload_getCurrent  # [code] &queue entries[cur]
func_80021ED4 preload_startRead  # [code] submits the chunk in slot 6 for the current entry (cd_readAsync, or cd_seekAsync for kind 1) with preload_onReadDone
func_80021FB4 preload_poll  # [code] > 0 while the current entry's request is running, -1 on error/unused
func_80022020 preload_finishRead  # [code] waits for preload_poll then preload_clearBusy
func_80022050 preload_clearBusy  # [code] clears flags 1 and 0x80 of the current entry
func_8002208C preload_waitCurrent  # [code] waits for the current entry's request and returns the entry
func_800220E8 preload_isReady  # [code] 1 when file id was preloaded (to RAM or VRAM), waiting for its read; game_loadModeOverlay uses it
func_800221C0 preload_overlaps  # [code] 1 when a RAM preload entry's destination overlaps [addr, addr + size)
func_80022280 preload_discardRange  # [code] waits for and clears every started RAM entry overlapping [addr, addr + size)
func_80022390 preload_remove  # [code] preload_find(id), wait, clear
func_800223CC preload_reset  # [code] waits for and clears every entry, state 4, count 0
func_80022B9C cd_abort  # [code] CdFlush, removes both callbacks, state 0
func_80022D3C cd_syncAsync  # [code] request flags 4: no CD command, the done callback runs once the queue drains (preload_wait spins on it)
func_80022E70 cd_sync  # [code] cd_syncAsync without callback, then waits for cd_getState() == 0
func_80022ED4 cd_seekStreamAsync  # [code] request flags 9 (seek with bit 8 = CdlSeekP in func_800233CC)
func_80022F1C cd_readStreamAsync  # [code] request flags 0xB (read with bit 8 = CdlReadS, the ready callback through func_800235A0); ovl_64 movie streaming
func_80022F64 cd_restartAt  # [code] sets the current request's sector, state 5 and pauses; the pause completion (func_8002350C) restarts the request
func_80023018 cd_pause  # [code] drops the queued request and pauses the drive (state 4)
func_800230A0 cd_isShellOpen  # [code] bit 4 (CdlStatShellOpen) of the last status byte in g_cdResult
func_8002325C cd_onSyncQueued  # [code] cd_onSync while a request is queued: completes the current request and starts the queued one
func_80023304 cd_onDiskError  # [code] cd_onSync for CdlDiskError: marks a retry (unk3), counts it, pauses
func_8002336C cd_onSetloc  # [code] after CdlSetloc: CdlSetmode (speed, size1) unless already set, else cd_startTransfer
func_800233CC cd_startTransfer  # [code] issues CdlReadN/CdlReadS (flag 2) or CdlSeekL/CdlSeekP for the current request
func_8002345C cd_onPaused  # [code] after CdlPause: completes the request (done callback), or restarts it when a retry is marked
func_8002350C cd_onRestart  # [code] restarts the current request (cd_restartAt's pause)
func_800235A0 cd_onStreamReady  # [code] ready callback for CdlReadS requests: pauses if a request is queued, else forwards to the request's own callback
